
============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS

--- RAG-IDB ---
what is the total number of patiemts who had crnry athrscl natve vssl?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "SINGLE" AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Int insert lead in vent"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'single'], ['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'int insert lead in vent']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Crnry athrscl natve vssl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'crnry athrscl natve vssl']]}
----------

----------
PREDS:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'ld body fluid']]}
----------
TRUTH:

calculate the number of dead patients who were admitted to hospital before 2123.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= "2123.0"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2123.0']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "1" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMITYEAR" < "2123"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."admityear"', '<', '2123'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '1']]}
----------

----------

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'personal history of other malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction'], ['demographic."status"', '=', 'alive']]}
----------
TRUTH:

PREDS:
SELECT LAB."LABEL",LAB."CATEGORY" FROM LAB WHERE LAB."SUBJECT_ID" = "2110"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['lab."label"', 'lab."category"'], 'tab': ['lab'], 'cond': [['lab."subject_id"', '=', '2110']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."NAME",LAB."CATEGORY" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = "2110"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."name"', 'lab."category"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."subject_id"', '=', '2110']]}
----------

let me know the diagnoses icd9 code and primary disease of patient with patient id 2110.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE", DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" FROM DIAGNOSES WHERE DIAGNOSES."SUBJECT_ID" = "2110"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['diagnoses."icd9_code"', 'diagnoses."short_title"'], 'tab': ['diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."subject_id"', '=', '2110']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS",DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = "2110"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."diagnosis"', 'diagnoses."icd9_code"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."subject_id"', '=', '2110']]}
----------

let me know the route of administration and the type of drug influenza virus vaccine is.
----------

----------

GOLD: SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMITYEAR" < "2174" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Cefpodoxime Proxetil"
PRED_OUT: Counter({(0,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(326,): 1})

GOLD: SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."INSURANCE" = "Self Pay" AND LAB."LABEL" = "RBC, CSF"
PRED_OUT: Counter({(35,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(33,): 1})

GOLD: SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "CONGESTIVE HEART FAILURE" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" > "13"
PRED_OUT: Counter({(10316,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(205,): 1})
